{"parentUuid":"29adec47-8a6a-499a-9f5f-15beffefaa6e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"568a3094-ff17-4144-8041-81815078475a","agentId":"ae619a3115cc0fa20","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0193tDKD8LRXrWomPLFn9iy1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Copyright (c) 2026 Raymond Manaloto\n2\t\"\"\"Architecture-scoped devcontainer resource names (#677).\n3\t\n4\tThe failure this whole module exists to prevent is *silent*: docker creates a\n5\tnamed volume on first mount, so two architectures sharing one home volume do\n6\tnot collide loudly — they interleave ``~/.local/share/mise/installs``,\n7\t``~/.cargo`` and ``~/.rustup`` until a binary of the wrong architecture is on\n8\tthe path. Every assertion below therefore checks that the arch word is\n9\tgenuinely *in* the name, not merely that two calls returned something.\n10\t\"\"\"\n11\t\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport hashlib\n15\t\n16\timport pytest\n17\tfrom dotfiles_setup.devcontainer_names import (\n18\t    NAME_FIELDS,\n19\t    SSH_PORT_BASE,\n20\t    SSH_PORT_ENV_VAR,\n21\t    SSH_PORT_SPAN,\n22\t    DevcontainerNames,\n23\t    HomeVolumeMigration,\n24\t    migration_platform_refusal,\n25\t    name_field,\n26\t    names_env,\n27\t    plan_home_volume_migration,\n28\t    resolve_names,\n29\t    ssh_port,\n30\t    workspace_hash,\n31\t)\n32\t\n33\tWORKSPACE = \"/workspaces/dotfiles\"\n34\tOTHER_WORKSPACE = \"/Users/dev/dotfiles\"\n35\tUSER = \"rmanaloto\"\n36\tAMD64 = \"linux/amd64/v2\"\n37\tARM64 = \"linux/arm64/v8\"\n38\t\n39\t\n40\tdef _names(\n41\t    workspace: str = WORKSPACE,\n42\t    platform: str = AMD64,\n43\t    port_override: str | int | None = None,\n44\t    env: dict[str, str] | None = None,\n45\t) -> DevcontainerNames:\n46\t    return resolve_names(\n47\t        workspace=workspace,\n48\t        user=USER,\n49\t        platform=platform,\n50\t        port_override=port_override,\n51\t        env=env,\n52\t    )\n53\t\n54\t\n55\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the hash\n56\t\n57\t\n58\tdef test_workspace_hash_is_the_deployed_shell_scheme() -> None:\n59\t    \"\"\"The python port must reproduce ``sha256sum | cut -c1-8`` exactly.\n60\t\n61\t    A different digest renames every existing volume on the next `mise run up`,\n62\t    which presents as an empty home rather than as an error.\n63\t    \"\"\"\n64\t    expected = hashlib.sha256(WORKSPACE.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]\n65\t    assert workspace_hash(WORKSPACE) == expected\n66\t\n67\t\n68\tdef test_workspace_hash_golden_values() -> None:\n69\t    \"\"\"Frozen goldens: an independent re-derivation cannot drift silently.\"\"\"\n70\t    assert workspace_hash(WORKSPACE) == \"be636185\"\n71\t    assert workspace_hash(OTHER_WORKSPACE) == \"01f20a06\"\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tdef test_workspace_hash_is_absolute_path_based() -> None:\n75\t    \"\"\"A relative path resolves, so `mise run up` from a symlink is stable.\"\"\"\n76\t    assert workspace_hash(WORKSPACE + \"/\") == workspace_hash(WORKSPACE)\n77\t\n78\t\n79\t# ------------------------------------------------------- the arch is in it\n80\t\n81\t\n82\t@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n83\t    (\"platform\", \"arch\"),\n84\t    [(AMD64, \"amd64\"), (ARM64, \"arm64\")],\n85\t)\n86\tdef test_container_and_volume_carry_the_architecture(platform: str, arch: str) -> None:\n87\t    names = _names(platform=platform)\n88\t    assert names.arch == arch\n89\t    # The arch must be its own dash-delimited segment, not an accidental\n90\t    # substring of the hash — `-amd64-` cannot be produced by a hex digest.\n91\t    assert f\"-{arch}-\" in names.container\n92\t    assert names.home_volume.endswith(f\"-{arch}-home\")\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef test_both_architectures_collide_on_nothing() -> None:\n96\t    \"\"\"AC: both architectures up simultaneously, no name/volume/port collision.\"\"\"\n97\t    amd = _names(platform=AMD64)\n98\t    arm = _names(platform=ARM64)\n99\t    assert amd.container != arm.container\n100\t    assert amd.home_volume != arm.home_volume\n101\t    assert amd.ssh_port != arm.ssh_port\n102\t\n103\t\n104\tdef test_volume_name_excludes_the_port() -> None:\n105\t    \"\"\"AC: changing a port must not orphan a home directory (C10/C11/C12).\"\"\"\n106\t    default = _names()\n107\t    moved = _names(port_override=45123)\n108\t    assert moved.ssh_port == 45123\n109\t    assert moved.home_volume == default.home_volume\n110\t    assert str(moved.ssh_port) not in moved.home_volume\n111\t\n112\t\n113\tdef test_container_name_includes_the_port() -> None:\n114\t    \"\"\"Two ports in one workspace+arch are two containers, not one.\"\"\"\n115\t    assert _names(port_override=45123).container.endswith(\"-45123\")\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tdef test_legacy_volume_is_the_pre_677_name() -> None:\n119\t    \"\"\"The migration source. If this drifts, the migration copies nothing.\"\"\"\n120\t    names = _names()\n121\t    assert names.legacy_home_volume == f\"dotfiles-dotfiles-{USER}-be636185-home\"\n122\t    assert names.arch not in names.legacy_home_volume\n123\t    assert names.legacy_home_volume != names.home_volume\n124\t\n125\t\n126\t# --------------------------------------------------------------- the port\n127\t\n128\t\n129\tdef test_second_working_directory_gets_a_distinct_port() -> None:\n130\t    \"\"\"AC: without manual configuration.\"\"\"\n131\t    here = _names(workspace=WORKSPACE)\n132\t    there = _names(workspace=OTHER_WORKSPACE)\n133\t    assert here.ssh_port != there.ssh_port\n134\t\n135\t\n136\tdef test_derived_port_is_deterministic_and_in_range() -> None:\n137\t    for workspace in (WORKSPACE, OTHER_WORKSPACE):\n138\t        for arch in (\"amd64\", \"arm64\"):\n139\t            port = ssh_port(workspace, arch)\n140\t            assert port == ssh_port(workspace, arch)\n141\t            assert SSH_PORT_BASE <= port < SSH_PORT_BASE + SSH_PORT_SPAN\n142\t\n143\t\n144\tdef test_derived_port_golden_values() -> None:\n145\t    \"\"\"Frozen: a change here silently moves every clone's SSH port.\"\"\"\n146\t    assert ssh_port(WORKSPACE, \"amd64\") == 21281\n147\t    assert ssh_port(WORKSPACE, \"arm64\") == 28455\n148\t\n149\t\n150\tdef test_derived_port_avoids_the_macos_ephemeral_range() -> None:\n151\t    \"\"\"The host hands out 49152-65535 to anonymous binds; a clash is a flaky R1.\"\"\"\n152\t    assert SSH_PORT_BASE + SSH_PORT_SPAN <= 49152\n153\t    assert SSH_PORT_BASE > 1024\n154\t\n155\t\n156\tdef test_explicit_override_wins_over_derivation() -> None:\n157\t    assert ssh_port(WORKSPACE, \"amd64\", override=\"4444\") == 4444\n158\t    assert _names(env={SSH_PORT_ENV_VAR: \"4444\"}).ssh_port == 4444\n159\t\n160\t\n161\tdef test_blank_override_falls_through_to_derivation() -> None:\n162\t test_unparsable_override_fails_loud() -> None:\n167\t    \"\"\"A typo'd port must not silently become a derived one.\"\"\"\n168\t    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"DEVCONTAINER_SSH_PORT\"):\n169\t        ssh_port(WORKSPACE, \"amd64\", override=\"forty-four-forty-four\")\n170\t\n171\t\n172\t# ------------------------------------------------------------- the exports\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tdef test_names_env_covers_every_substitution_devcontainer_json_makes() -> None:\n176\t    env = names_env(_names())\n177\t    assert set(env) == {\n178\t        \"DEVCONTAINER_WORKSPACE_HASH\",\n179\t        \"DEVCONTAINER_ARCH\",\n180\t        \"DEVCONTAINER_NAME\",\n181\t        \"DEVCONTAINER_HOME_VOLUME\",\n182\t        SSH_PORT_ENV_VAR,\n183\t    }\n184\t    assert env[\"DEVCONTAINER_NAME\"] == _names().container\n185\t    assert env[\"DEVCONTAINER_HOME_VOLUME\"] == _names().home_volume\n186\t\n187\t\n188\tdef test_name_field_addresses_every_field() -> None:\n189\t    names = _names()\n190\t    for field in NAME_FIELDS:\n191\t        assert name_field(field, names)\n192\t    assert name_field(\"port\", names) == str(names.ssh_port)\n193\t\n194\t\n195\tdef test_name_field_rejects_an_unknown_field() -> None:\n196\t    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"unknown devcontainer name field\"):\n197\t        name_field(\"hostname\", _names())\n198\t\n199\t\n200\t# ---------------------------------------------------------- the migration\n201\t\n202\t\n203\tdef test_migration_is_skipped_when_the_legacy_volume_is_gone() -> None:\n204\t    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(_names(), existing_volumes=())\n205\t    assert plan.action == \"nothing-to-migrate\"\n206\t    assert plan.commands == ()\n207\t\n208\t\n209\tdef test_migration_is_skipped_when_the_target_already_has_content() -> None:\n210\t    \"\"\"Re-running after a completed migration must not copy over live state.\"\"\"\n211\t    names = _names()\n212\t    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(\n213\t        names,\n214\t ()\n219\t\n220\t\n221\tdef test_migration_resumes_over_an_interrupted_first_attempt() -> None:\n222\t    \"\"\"AC: an interrupted first creation leaves a recoverable state.\n223\t\n224\t    An empty-but-existing target is exactly what an interrupted copy leaves\n225\t    behind, so it must be treated as work to redo, never as work already done.\n226\t    \"\"\"\n227\t    names = _names()\n228\t    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(\n229\t        names,\n230\t        existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume, names.home_volume),\n231\t        target_populated=False,\n232\t    )\n233\t    assert plan.action == \"copy\"\n234\t    assert plan.commands\n235\t\n236\t\n237\tdef test_migration_copies_from_legacy_into_the_arch_scoped_volume() -> None:\n238\t    names = _names()\n239\t    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(\n240\t        names, existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume,)\n241\t    )\n242\t    assert isinstance(plan, HomeVolumeMigration)\n243\t    assert plan.action == \"copy\"\n244\t    joined = [\" \".join(cmd) for cmd in plan.commands]\n245\t    copy = next(line for line in joined if \"cp\" in line)\n246\t    # Direction matters: reversing these two overwrites the surviving home.\n247\t    assert f\"{names.legacy_home_volume}:/from:ro\" in copy\n248\t    assert f\"{names.home_volume}:/to\" in copy\n249\t    assert copy.index(\":/from\") < copy.index(\":/to\")\n250\t\n251\t\n252\tdef test_migration_refuses_to_guess_an_architecture() -> None:\n253\t    \"\"\"Measured hazard, not theory.\n254\t\n255\t    ``dotfiles-setup devcontainer env`` resolves ``amd64`` under ``mise run``\n256\t    (which supplies the repo pin) and ``arm64`` from a bare shell on this\n257\t    M-series Mac. The pre-#677 volume name records no architecture, so an\n258\t    unpinned copy would name its target for whichever machine happened to ask.\n259\t    \"\"\"\n260\t    refusal = migration_platform_refusal(None, env={})\n261\t    assert refusal is not None\n262\t    assert \"DOTFILES_PLATFORM\" in refusal\n263\t\n264\t\n265\tdef test_migration_proceeds_when_the_platform_is_pinned() -> None:\n266\t    assert migration_platform_refusal(None, env={\"DOTFILES_PLATFORM\": AMD64}) is None\n267\t\n268\t\n269\tdef test_migration_proceeds_when_the_platform_is_explicit() -> None:\n270\t    \"\"\"An explicit --platform beats an unset environment.\"\"\"\n271\t    assert migration_platform_refusal(ARM64, env={}) is None\n272\t\n273\t\n274\tdef test_blank_platform_pin_is_not_a_pin() -> None:\n275\t    \"\"\"An unset variable renders as '', which must not read as an answer.\"\"\"\n276\t    blank = migration_platform_refusal(None, env={\"DOTFILES_PLATFORM\": \"  \"})\n277\t    assert blank is not None\n278\t\n279\t\n280\tdef test_migration_refuses_while_a_container_still_holds_the_source() -> None:\n281\t    \"\"\"A live home is being WRITTEN to; `cp -a` would capture a torn record.\n282\t\n283\t    Measured on the real host: the pre-#677 volume was 3.5 GB and mounted\n284\t    read-write by a running container. A torn home starts fine and misbehaves\n285\t    later, which is the worst shape of all — so refuse and say `mise run stop`.\n286\t    \"\"\"\n287\t    names = _names()\n288\t    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(\n289\t        names,\n290\t        existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume,),\n291\t        source_in_use=True,\n292\t    )\n293\t    assert plan.action == \"source-in-use\"\n294\t    assert plan.commands == ()\n295\t    assert \"mise run stop\" in plan.reason\n296\t\n297\t\n298\tdef test_in_use_refusal_outranks_nothing_to_do() -> None:\n299\t    \"\"\"The refusal must not be reachable only when there is work; and a plain\n300\t    absent source stays a no-op rather than becoming an error.\"\"\"\n301\t    names = _names()\n302\t    idle = plan_home_volume_migration(\n303\t        names, existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume,), source_in_use=False\n304\t    )\n305\t    assert idle.action == \"copy\"\n306\t    absent = plan_home_volume_migration(\n307\t        names, existing_volumes=(), source_in_use=True\n308\t    )\n309\t    assert absent.action == \"nothing-to-migrate\"\n310\t\n311\t\n312\tdef test_refused_actions_are_distinguishable_from_nothing_to_do() -> None:\n313\t    \"\"\"`--apply` exits non-zero on a refusal and zero on a genuine no-op.\n314\t\n315\t    Both produce an empty command list, so without this split a refusal under\n316\t    `--apply` would look exactly like success to any caller reading the rc.\n317\t    \"\"\"\n318\t    assert \"source-in-use\" in _REFUSED_ACTIONS\n319\t    assert \"nothing-to-migrate\" not in _REFUSED_ACTIONS\n320\t    assert \"already-migrated\" not in _REFUSED_ACTIONS\n321\t\n322\t\n323\tdef test_migration_never_deletes_the_legacy_volume() -> None:\n324\t    \"\"\"The source is the only copy until the user is satisfied; `prune` removes it.\"\"\"\n325\t    names = _names()\n326\t    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(\n327\t        names, existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume,)\n328\t    )\n329\t    assert not any(\"rm\" in \" \".join(cmd).split() for cmd in plan.commands)\n330\t"}]},"uuid":"ee10bf25-b13f-4d46-9dac-aefbab72596e","timestamp":"2026-08-10T11:35:42.815Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"29adec47-8a6a-499a-9f5f-15beffefaa6e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/rmanaloto/dev/github/ray-manaloto/dotfiles","sessionId":"eeaf6a41-3473-4c9e-a681-cca17051c6ab","version":"2.1.226","gitBranch":"feat/677-arch-scoped-devcontainer-names"}

A torn home starts fine and misbehaves\n137\t+    later, which is the worst shape of all — so refuse and say `mise run stop`.\n138\t+    \"\"\"\n139\t+    names = _names()\n140\t+    plan = plan_home_volume_migration(\n141\t+        names,\n142\t+        existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume,),\n143\t+        source_in_use=True,\n144\t+    )\n145\t+    assert plan.action == \"source-in-use\"\n146\t+    assert plan.commands == ()\n147\t+    assert \"mise run stop\" in plan.reason\n148\t+\n149\t+\n150\t+def test_in_use_refusal_outranks_nothing_to_do() -> None:\n151\t+    \"\"\"Both arms of the in-use flag, on both source states.\n152\t+\n153\t+    The refusal must not be reachable only when there is work, and a plain\n154\t+    absent source must stay a no-op rather than becoming an error.\n155\t+    \"\"\"\n156\t+    names = _names()\n157\t+    idle = plan_home_volume_migration(\n158\t+        names, existing_volumes=(names.legacy_home_volume,), source_in_use=False\n159\t+    )\n160\t+    assert idle.action == \"copy\"\n161\t+    absent = plan_home_volume_migration(names, existing_volumes=(), source_in_use=True)\n162\t+    assert absent.action == \"nothing-to-migrate\"\n163\t+\n164\t+\n165\t+def test_refused_actions_are_distinguishable_from_nothing_to_do() -> None:\n166\t+    \"\"\"`--apply` exits non-zero on a refusal and zero on a genuine no-op.\n167\t+\n168\t+    Both produce an empty command list, so without this split a refusal under\n169\t+    `--apply` would look exactly like success to any caller reading the rc.\n170\t+    \"\"\"\n171\t+    assert \"source-in-use\" in REFUSED_ACTIONS\n172\t+    assert \"nothing-to-migrate\" not in REFUSED_ACTIONS\n173\t+    assert \"already-migrated\" not in REFUSED_ACTIONS\n174\t+\n175\t+\n176\t def test_migration_never_deletes_the_legacy_volume() -> None:\n177\t     \"\"\"The source is the only copy until the user is satisfied; {"parentUuid":"490ec790-5b8c-4fee-a917-68d60da14949","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"568a3094-ff17-4144-8041-81815078475a","agentId":"ae619a3115cc0fa20","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HGTFeCPrfWvHEsenXvMRBs","type":"tool_result","content":"100:    \"--name\",\n213:  \"appPort\": [\"${localEnv:DEVCONTAINER_SSH_PORT}:2222\"],\n227:  \"customizations\": {\n---\n  // `mise run up` evals `dotfiles-setup devcontainer env`, which derives all\n  // of them — including the architecture word, which must come from\n  // platform_target and never from a literal (`no_platform_literals`). A JSON\n  // string is also the one place a reviewer cannot see an arch is missing.\n  \"name\": \"${localEnv:DEVCONTAINER_NAME}\",\n  \"build\": {\n    \"dockerfile\": \"Dockerfile.host-user\",\n    // #673: the platform is ONE parameter — mise.toml's global\n    // [env] DOTFILES_PLATFORM. `${localEnv:...}` interpolates inside array\n    // elements (same mechanism as runArgs below). Deliberately WITHOUT a\n    // `:default` fallback: a fallback here would be a second platform\n    // literal, and the whole point is that exactly one place chooses. Bring\n    // the container up via `mise run up`, which supplies it.\n    \"options\": [\"--platform=${localEnv:DOTFILES_PLATFORM}\"],\n    \"args\": {\n      \"DEVCONTAINER_USER\": \"${localEnv:USER}\",\n      \"BASE_IMAGE\": \"${localEnv:BASE_IMAGE}\",\n    },\n  },\n  \"runArgs\": [\n    \"--name\",\n    \"${localEnv:DEVCONTAINER_NAME}\",\n    \"--env-file\",\n    \"${localEnv:HOME}/.local/state/dotfiles/doppler.env\",\n    \"--platform=${localEnv:DOTFILES_PLATFORM}\",\n  ],\n  \"remoteUser\": \"${localEnv:USER}\",\n  \"workspaceFolder\": \"/workspaces/${localWorkspaceFolderBasename}\",\n  \"workspaceMount\": \"source=${localWorkspaceFolder},target=/workspaces/${localWorkspaceFolderBasename},type=bind,consistency=cached\",\n  \"mounts\": [\n    \"source=${localEnv:HOME}/.local/state/dotfiles,target=/tmp/dotfiles-host-state,type=bind\",\n    \"source=/run/host-services/ssh-auth.sock,target=/run/host-services/ssh-auth.sock,type=bind\",\n    // Architecture-scoped (#677). The home volume carries compiled output —\n    // ~/.local/share/mise/installs, ~/.cargo, ~/.rustup — so sharing one\n    // across architectures interleaves binaries silently: docker reuses a\n    // named volume on mount and reports nothing.\n    \"source=${localEnv:DEVCONTAINER_HOME_VOLUME},target=/home/${localEnv:USER},type=volume\",\n  ],\n  \"containerEnv\": {\n    // ─ Platform hints ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    // Runtime visibility for user shells (e.g., when a user invokes\n    // conda directly without going through mise). Also set in\n    // .devcontainer/mise-system.toml [env] which applies during\n    // build-time `mise install --system -y` (when containerEnv has not\n    // yet been applied to the build process). Duplication is intentional\n    // — the two windows do not overlap.\n    \"MISE_ARCH\": \"x86_64\",\n    \"MISE_OS\": \"linux\",\n    \"CONDA_SUBDIR\": \"linux-64\",\n\n    // ─ Container-private python venv (review finding [33], 2026-07-07) ─","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"cb3c27fb-30e2-41d7-b165-86fb596ead0c","timestamp":"2026-08-10T11:36:59.231Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"490ec790-5b8c-4fee-a917-68d60da14949","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/rmanaloto/dev/github/ray-manaloto/dotfiles","sessionId":"eeaf6a41-3473-4c9e-a681-cca17051c6ab","version":"2.1.226","gitBranch":"feat/677-arch-scoped-devcontainer-names"}

It covers the entire user\n-home, so `~/.cache/mise`, `~/.cache/uv`, `~/.bash_history`,\n-`~/.ssh/known_hosts`, and TMPDIR all persist across `stop/up`.\n+## Dynamic Naming (v7 architecture-scoped, #677)\n+\n+Names are **resolved, never composed in config**: `mise run up` evals\n+`dotfiles-setup devcontainer env` (`python/src/dotfiles_setup/devcontainer_names.py`)\n+and `devcontainer.json` interpolates whole values. Distinct\n+names let docker's own uniqueness enforce it.\n+\n+**Migrating a pre-#677 volume:** `mise run migrate-home-volume` (dry-run;\n+`-- --apply` executes) copies `…-<hash>-home` into `…-<hash>-<arch>-home` and\n+**never deletes the source** — `mise run prune` does, once the new container is\n+known good. Run it via mise: a bare CLI call *refuses*, because the old name\n+records no arch and an unpinned resolve would take one from the host.\n+\n+It covers the whole user home, so `~/.cache/mise`, `~/.cache/uv`,\n+`~/.bash_history`, `~/.ssh/known_hosts` and TMPDIR persist across `stop/up`.\n+The v5 per-directory volumes (`mise-user`, `cargo-user`, `rustup-user`) it\n+replaced are orphans; `mise run prune` cleans them.\n \n **TMPDIR persistence:** `Dockerfile.host-user` sets\n `ENV TMPDIR=/home/${USER}/.local/tmp` on the home volume.\n `on-create.sh` sweeps files older than 30 days (atime) and prunes\n empty directories per container create to bound growth.\n \n-**Accepted trade-off — data loss on rollout:** First `mise run up`\n-after the v5→v6 change orphans the old volumes; runtime-installed\n-tools/crates/toolchains must be re-installed. `mise run prune` cleans\n-orphans. See\n+`.claude/rules/md-size-budgets.md`.\n+\n+User-overlay paths live on the single **architecture-scoped** home volume\n+(`dotfiles-<basename>-<user>-<hash>-<arch>-home`, #677); `mise run stop &&\n+mise run up` preserves all state. Since v6 that includes `~/.cache/uv`,\n+`~/.local/tmp` (TMPDIR, 30-day atime sweep in `on-create.sh`) and\n+`~/.bash_history`.\n+\n+| Tool family | System install (baked) | User overlay | How to add system |\n+|---|---|---|---|\n+| mise tools | `/usr/local/share/mise/installs/` | `~/.local/share/mise/installs/` | `mise-system.toml` (base) / `mise-runtime.toml` (runtime) + image PR; overlay tier: `home/dot_config/mise/config.toml.tmpl` |\n+| cargo crates | `/usr/local/share/cargo/{bin,registry}` | `~/.cargo/{bin,registry}` | base image PR; runtime `cargo install` |\n+| rust toolchains | `/usr/local/share/rustup/toolchains/` | `~/.rustup/toolchains/` | `mise-system.toml` `rust = \"...\"`; runtime `rustup install` |\n+| pipx tools | `/usr/local/share/mise/installs/pipx-*` | shadowed by mise overlay | `\"pipx:<name>\"` in `mise-system.toml` |\n+| apt packages | `/usr/{bin,lib,share}/...` | **none — not persistable** | `mise-system.toml [bootstrap.packages]` + base image PR |\n+\n+**Apt packages have no runtime persistence.** Add system packages to\n+`mise-system.toml [bootstrap.packages]` and ship via a base-image PR.\n+`sudo apt install` at runtime works but is lost on container recreate.\n+\n+**Why the overlay column matters for #677.** Every path in it holds *compiled\n+output*. Before the home volume carried the architecture, an amd64 and an arm64\n+container in the same working directory mounted the same volume and interleaved\n+these trees — docker reuses a named volume on mount and reports nothing, so the\n+first symptom was an `exec format error` far from its cause.\ndiff --git a/AGENTS.md b/AGENTS.md\nindex a657caf..a0948bb 100644\n--- a/AGENTS.md\n+++ b/AGENTS.md\n@@ -156,7 +156,7 @@ Gated by `mise run verify-local`. Pin per-clone via `mise.local.toml`. Volume names still exclude the port (C10/C11/C12). |\n | `DOTFILES_PLATFORM` | pinned in `mise.toml` `[env]` | **The one platform parameter** (#673). Every `--platform` site resolves from it; unset, it falls back to the host's native triple. `no_platform_literals` rejects a literal elsewhere |\n | `DOCKER_DEFAULT_PLATFORM` | `{{ env.DOTFILES_PLATFORM }}` | Task-scoped export of the above — what docker itself reads |\n \ndiff --git a/docs/specs/devcontainer-gcc162-dual-arch.md b/docs/specs/devcontainer-gcc162-dual-arch.md\nindex f52b1cb..6be0fed 100644\n--- a/docs/specs/devcontainer-gcc162-dual-arch.md\n+++ b/docs/specs/devcontainer-gcc162-dual-arch.md\n@@ -1535,6 +1535,35 @@ identical on a Struct. **22 lines replace 60: a net reduction.**\n \n ---\n \n+### D32 — #677: names carry the arch; the port is DERIVED; the old home is COPIED\n+\n+Ray, 2026-08-10, asked and answered before implementation:\n+\n+- **SSH port** — derived from workspace **and** architecture into 20000-29999\n+  (`DEVCONTAINER_SSH_PORT` still pins one per clone), *and* this clone gets a\n+  gitignored `mise.local.toml` pinning **4444**, so the amd64 devloop keeps the\n+  port every doc and ssh config already knows. Chosen over \"keep 4444 as a\n+  base + offset\", which would have preserved the *appearance* of continuity\n+  (1% chance of actually landing on 4444) with a 200-slot collision space.\n+- **Home volume** — a one-shot `mise run migrate-home-volume` **copies**\n+  `…-<hash>-home` into `…-<hash>-<arch>-home` rather than accepting the v5→v6\n+  precedent of orphan-and-reinstall. The source is never deleted; `mise run\n+  prune` removes it once the new container is known good. Accepted at ask time:\n+  the copy needs a raw `docker run` (adjacent to `do-not.md` #3 — it is a data\n+  copy, not devcontainer lifecycle) and a half-finished copy is a new state to\n+  handle, which is why an existing-but-**empty** target reads as *resume*, never\n+  as *done*.\n+- **Measured hazard that shaped the CLI:** `dotfiles-setup devcontainer env`\n+  resolves `amd64` under `mise run` (the repo pin) and `arm64` from a bare\n+  shell on this M-series Mac. The pre-#677 volume name records no architecture,\n+  so the migration **refuses** without an explicit platform rather than naming\n+  its target for whichever machine happened to ask.\n+\n+Consequence for R1: the documented probe is now\n+`ssh ${USER}@localhost -p $(mise run ssh-port)`, not a literal 4444.\n+\n+---\n+\n ## ✅ Promoted to `docs/specs/` — tracked, survives a clone\n \n Done 2026-08-08 on Ray's instruction (Q17). The whole chain was also control-armed against the REAL binary (no graph → `graph file not found`, rc 1) |\n | `test_image_smoke.py` | pytest | Smoke-test script generation, `_parse_human_size`, the #143 tool-set jq/diff block (host-bash), and the #223 shared cores — tier-1 (`build_tier1_script`, HEAD-vs-merge-base identity resolvers, a real bash EXECUTION test that the identity loop gates on a wrong hash) and tier-3 (`build_tier3_script` sanitizer+reflection substrate, `resolve_expected_p2996_ref_at_base` merge-base ref-pin, TSan-skip/ref-strict injection, golden byte-parity with `build_smoke_script`; #294 adds the FUNCTIONAL apt-LLVM-22 probes — the control-armed default-clang identity+version gate (`_TIER3_DEFAULT_CLANG`), `-fopenmp`/`-fuse-ld=lld`/flang compile+run, libclc bitcode, and `--version` smoke for opt/llc/llvm-cov/llvm-profdata/llvm-symbolizer/llvm-bolt/mlir-opt, with the version injected merge-base-aware via `resolve_expected_llvm_version[_at_base]`); plus the #231 image-analysis resolver (`decide_analysis_target` FAIL/quiet-skip, `_lookup_pr_number` empty-array trap, `resolve_analysis_ref_main`) and scope-(b) benchmark instrumentation (`classify_layer_source`, `estimate_pull_time_s`, `compare_payloads`, trend summary); #674 adds the manifest-index architecture selection — six FAIL arms proving a mismatch RAISES rather than silently measuring the local image (absent arch, attestations-only, matching arch on another os, empty index, unreadable SELECTED sub-manifest) against armed fixtures whose two architectures carry different layer totals |","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"a8e4189f-6a0a-43d7-9cde-f216e818fe55","timestamp":"2026-08-10T11:37:10.789Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e9101e6c-0cd9-470c-9550-3b4d5f7e84a3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/rmanaloto/dev/github/ray-manaloto/dotfiles","sessionId":"eeaf6a41-3473-4c9e-a681-cca17051c6ab","version":"2.1.226","gitBranch":"feat/677-arch-scoped-devcontainer-names"}

{"parentUuid":"b36ffb31-bb6f-495b-8cb3-a7a891fa69bd","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ae619a3115cc0fa20","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cdu1Ejz8Lz2VCD5a6CwHu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"`ReportFindings` is not available in this environment (not in the tool list and not resolvable via ToolSearch), so the findings are recorded in `.agent/notepad.md` and restated below.\n\n**Scope:** `git diff main...HEAD` at `548460e` (#677, arch-scoped devcontainer names) plus the working-tree deltas that landed mid-review (the `source-in-use` migration guard). The tree changed under me once; I re-snapshotted and reviewed the current state.\n\n## Findings\n\n**`mise.toml:296` — HIGH.** `devcontainer up --workspace-folder .` is invoked without `--id-label`. In the pinned CLI (`@devcontainers/cli` 0.88.0, `devContainersSpecCLI.js`, function `bg`) an existing container is looked up **only** by `devcontainer.local_folder=<folder>` + `devcontainer.config_file=<config>` — both per-folder, neither arch-aware — and `--help` confirms \"If no `--id-label` is given, one will be inferred from the `--workspace-folder` path.\" So the new arch-scoped `--name` never causes a second container to be created: with amd64 up, `mise run up` targeting arm64 finds the amd64 container by label and reuses it, reporting success. The AC \"both architectures up simultaneously in one working directory\" is not met. The immediately reproducible case is rollout: any user with a pre-#677 container running gets it silently reused, after which `verify-arch` and `verify-ssh-inbound` fail against the new name/port with no hint that a `mise run stop` / `dev-rebuild` is required. Fix direction: add an arch-bearing `--id-label` to `up`/`dev-rebuild` (and to the `exec`-based tasks).\n\n**`.devcontainer/devcontainer.json:126` — HIGH.** `containerEnv` still hard-codes `\"MISE_ARCH\": \"x86_64\"` and `\"CONDA_SUBDIR\": \"linux-64\"` (mirrored at `mise-system.toml:351,353`). This PR's premise is that an arm64 container can now come up in the same directory; when it does, mise resolves x86_64 artifacts and conda resolves `linux-64` packages *into the correctly arch-scoped arm64 home volume* — producing the `exec format error` the module docstring and `TOOL-PERSISTENCE.md` say the change exists to prevent, just relocated from the volume name to the env. `no_platform_literals` cannot see it (it matches `linux/<arch>`, not a bare `x86_64`), which is the same blind spot #698 hit with the surviving `MISE_ARCH` pin.\n\n**`mise.toml:289` (and `:331`) — MEDIUM.** `eval \"$(uv run --project python dotfiles-setup devcontainer env)\"` discards the resolver's exit status: under `set -euo pipefail`, `eval` of the empty string returns 0. Control-armed: `x=\"$(sh -c 'exit 3')\"` aborts with rc=3, while `eval \"$(sh -c 'exit 3')\"` continues and the script exits 0. Concrete scenario: a typo'd `DEVCONTAINER_SSH_PORT` in `mise.local.toml` makes `ssh_port` raise (its documented \"fails loud\" behaviour), `devcontainer env` writes nothing to stdout, `up` proceeds, and `devcontainer.json` substitutes empty strings for `DEVCONTAINER_NAME` and `DEVCONTAINER_HOME_VOLUME` — the home mount loses its named source. Assign to a variable first, then `eval` it.\n\n**`python/src/dotfiles_setup/devcontainer_names.py:318` — MEDIUM.** `plan_home_volume_migration` tests `target_populated` before anything else, and the docstring's recovery story only covers a target that is *empty*. A copy interrupted partway (the notepad records the real volume at 3.5 GB) leaves a **non-empty** target, so the next run returns `already-migrated` with \"copying over it would destroy live state\" — the user is told the migration is done and brings the container up on a truncated home. That is the same torn-home failure the new `source_in_use` guard was added to prevent, arriving through the other door.\n\n**`mise.toml:373, 378, 447, 962, 1015` — MEDIUM.** `verify-arch` was fixed to select by container name, with a comment stating that `devcontainer.local_folder` \"is per-FOLDER, so once both architectures can be up in one directory it matches TWO containers.\" But `smoke`, `sh`, and the three `devcontainer exec` calls in `persistence` resolve through the same per-folder id labels (`bg` again), and `stop`/`prune` filter `docker ps -aq --filter label=devcontainer.local_folder=$PWD`. With two containers up, `mise run smoke` can smoke the other architecture and `mise run stop` tears down both — including mid-`persistence`, which then brings only one back.\n\n**`python/src/dotfiles_setup/devcontainer_names.py:117` — LOW.** `workspace_hash` hashes `Path(workspace).resolve()` (the *physical* path) while the retired `scripts/workspace-hash.sh` hashed `$PWD` (the *logical* path bash preserves when you `cd` through a symlink). The docstring claims byte-for-byte compatibility; for a workspace reached via a symlink the two digests differ, which the same docstring says \"reads as an empty home, not as an error.\" `test_workspace_hash_is_absolute_path_based` claims in its docstring to cover \"up from a symlink\" but only varies a trailing slash — a probe that cannot fail for the reason it states. (No divergence on this host; the risk is other clones.)\n\n**`python/src/dotfiles_setup/main.py:381` — LOW.** The insertion point put `_add_devcontainer_subcommands` *before* the tail of `_add_honesty_subcommands`, so the new function (381–546) also registers `classifier-axes`, `lock-check`, `lock-tools`, `doctor`, `image-lock` and others. Functionally identical — same `subparsers` object — but the docstring says it registers \"`devcontainer env|name|migrate-home`\", and the stated reason for the function (keeping the caller under ruff's statement cap) is achieved by moving unrelated registrations into it.\n\n**`python/src/dotfiles_setup/devcontainer_names.py:141` — LOW.** The `DEVCONTAINER_SSH_PORT` override is parsed with a bare `int()`, so `0`, `70000` and `-1` pass the guard whose docstring promises \"a typo'd port must not silently become a derived one.\" The failure surfaces later as a docker port-binding error rather than at the resolver. `int()` also accepts underscores, so `4_444` silently becomes 4444.\n\n## Notes on what checked out\n\n- `bash_budget.py` allowlist entry removed in step with the deleted script; no stale references to `scripts/workspace-hash.sh` outside archived research.\n- `pr.py` `SURFACE_PATTERNS` correctly picks up the new module, and `mise.toml` was already covered.\n- The blank-override-falls-through-to-derivation path is right: mise renders unset vars as `\"\"`, and both `up` and `verify-ssh-inbound` template to `''`, so the derivation is reached.\n- The migration never emits a `rm`, and the `source-in-use` refusal exits non-zero only under `--apply` — both correct."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":880,"cache_read_input_tokens":187429,"output_tokens":2649,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":880},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2649,"cache_read_input_tokens":187429,"cache_creation_input_tokens":880,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":880,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cdu1EhK9do4e597Jsdgsj","attributionSkill":"code-review","type":"assistant","uuid":"c910a53c-9625-425a-be8b-0263d230850a","timestamp":"2026-08-10T11:41:06.568Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/rmanaloto/dev/github/ray-manaloto/dotfiles","sessionId":"eeaf6a41-3473-4c9e-a681-cca17051c6ab","version":"2.1.226","gitBranch":"feat/677-arch-scoped-devcontainer-names"}
